A complaint was filed by Interdamo S.A. against ITM Belgium S.A. and SCA Produits régionaux on 18 January 2002 and was registered with the secretariat of the Competition Council under reference CONC-P/K-02/0007. The complainant is an undertaking within the meaning of Article 1(a) of the Act, which had a direct and current interest at the time of filing the complaint and the application for provisional measures. Indeed, as a member of the franchise network set up by ITM Belgium S.A. and its subsidiaries, it considers that it has been harmed by the establishment of an agreement prohibited by Article 2 of the Act by means of these membership and sign contracts. These would contain provisions that would have the object of limiting the commercial policy, not only of the interested party, but also of the other members. Interdamo S.A. further asserts that it has experienced significant difficulties since 28 November 2001 following the prohibition imposed on its managing director from attending a monthly meeting of franchisees at ITM's headquarters. These difficulties result, according to the applicant for provisional measures, from the failure by ITM to transmit important information necessary for the operation of its point of sale (such as the prices of a series of goods) and from delivery schedules not being respected. ITM S.A. contests the admissibility of the present application on the ground that parallel proceedings have been brought before the courts of the Judicial Order and the national competition authorities concerning a dispute and questions that appear to be if not identical, at least similar. The existence of such concurrent proceedings does not preclude the jurisdiction of the Competition Council and its President insofar as neither the thing claimed, nor the law invoked, nor the parties involved are the same: the actions operate on different planes that do not overlap (Explanatory Memorandum, Parl. Doc., Ch. Repr., Ord. Sess. 1989-1990, No. 1282/1, pp. 33, 34). Various decisions on provisional measures pronounced in accordance with Article 35 of the Act of 5 August 1991 on the Protection of Economic Competition explain why the risk of contradiction does not exist since the grounds for the decisions pronounced by the Courts and Tribunals on the one hand, and by the Council on the other hand are necessarily different. The Act on the Protection of Economic Competition alone is capable of founding a decision of the Competition Council and its president. The President of the Competition Council may thus, by virtue of Article 35 of the Act, take provisional measures intended to suspend the restrictive competition practices that are the subject of an investigation, if it is urgent to avoid a situation likely to cause serious, imminent and irreparable harm to the undertakings whose interests are affected by these practices or to harm the general economic interest, notwithstanding the existence of any judicial proceedings or decisions. If, in the context of the proceedings conducted in parallel, the operative part of the application made by Interdamo is similar, the intervention of the Competition Council - and of its president in the context of provisional measures - takes place within the framework of objective litigation concerning legality and the protection of economic competition and not within the framework of subjective litigation settling particular disputes or establishing the right of a party. The interests protected are therefore above all public and not private, which moreover justifies the erga omnes nature of the Council's decisions which must be published in the Moniteur belge pursuant to Article 41 of the Act, unlike decisions pronounced by the Courts and Tribunals of the Judicial Order. Furthermore, the (President of the) Council, when called upon to rule on the particular subject matter falling within its jurisdiction, has in particular the reasoned report of the Corps of Rapporteurs drawn up at the end of a thorough investigation into the possible existence of a violation of the rules of economic competition, which the courts of the Judicial Order called upon to rule on possible violations of subjective rights do not have. Being courts belonging to distinct orders, the material jurisdictions do not overlap and the decisions have a fundamentally different scope. The application for provisional measures brought by Interdamo S.A. is consequently admissible. 2. Prima facie existence of an infringement of the Act (

### article 2)

Given the specificity of the procedure provided for by article 35 LPCE, it is necessary to verify whether, on the basis of the elements of the investigation file available, there exists prima facie an infringement of the law of 5 August 1991 on the protection of economic competition. The alleged infringement must thus have a plausible character: it is however not necessary at this stage of the procedure to establish the existence of an infringement of the competition rules with the same degree of certainty as that required for a decision on the merits. The examination of the investigation file and the documents of the procedure reveals that S.A. Interdamo merely invokes grievances and advances assertions without providing elements of proof establishing the existence of a prima facie infringement of the law of 5 August 1991 on the protection of economic competition. This applicant party for provisional measures has moreover not deemed it necessary to appear at the hearings of the Competition Council to substantiate its accusations and has even notified by fax on the day of the hearing fixed to enable it to develop its grievances that it did not intend to pursue its application for provisional measures. It must moreover be noted that S.A. ITM has produced documents (and in particular advertisements of members including those emanating from the applicant for provisional measures), demonstrating on the basis of the current elements of the file that these grievances are not relevant. Nothing permitting, at this stage, to conclude that there exists a prima facie infringement, there is reason to note the absence of grounds for the application for provisional measures filed by S.A. Interdamo. Given that one of the conditions for the granting of provisional measures is lacking, there is no interest in examining those relating to serious and irreparable harm, and to urgency or that aimed at verifying whether the general economic interest is not harmed. For these reasons,
We, Patrick DE WOLF, vice-president of the Competition Council acting as president,
Declare the application for provisional measures filed by S.A. Interdamo and registered under the reference CONC-V/M-02/0008 admissible but unfounded; Thus decided on 13 June 2002. FEDERAL PUBLIC SERVICE FINANCE

FEDERAL PUBLIC SERVICE PERSONNEL AND ORGANISATION
[C − 2003/02024]
4 FEBRUARY 2003. — Circular No. 536 — Social programming standards applicable to common grades at level D and to salary scale 22B

To the federal public services and the services dependent on them, to the Ministry of Defence, as well as to the public interest bodies belonging to the federal administrative public service as defined in article 1 of the law of 22 July 1993 containing certain measures regarding the public service
Madam Minister, Mr Minister, Mr Secretary of State,
A. Social programming standards in level D
The social programming standards in level D are increased in the framework of the career reform. The increased standards are calculated on the total of:
-	the statutory staff remunerated on 31 December 2001 in the first and second salary scales of level 3; -	the number of posts fixed at that time in the ministerial order implementing the staff establishment in the salary scales subject to standards, except in case of surplus. In that case, the number of statutory staff on 31 December 2001 is taken into consideration. In order to know the definitive number of posts per salary scale in level D, the following social programming standards are applied to this total:
1. for the grade of administrative collaborator:
-	27% in salary scale DA2; -	31% in salary scale DA3; -	12% in salary scale DA4; 2. for the grade of technical collaborator:
-	25% in salary scale DT3; -	35% in salary scale DT4; -	10% in salary scale DT5. If the number of persons as a result of integration into the new career were to exceed the number of posts per salary scale, the surplus persists. The surplus therefore does not become the new standard. A recalculation of these numbers is only possible in case of significant staff transfers in the framework of a restructuring or transfer of competences or in case of negotiations on particular grades insofar as they have been integrated into the common grades. B. Social programming standard for access to salary scale 22B
The officials who are integrated into the new salary scales on 1 June 2002 and who are, at that time, remunerated in salary scales 20E and 22A, may, as a transitional measure, be promoted to salary scale 22B in extinction. This promotion to 22B in extinction depends on the number of vacant posts provided for in this salary scale. The total number of posts linked to this salary scale 22B is fixed at 25% of the number of permanent officials on 31 May 2002, remunerated in salary scales 20E, 22A and 22B, and, after 31 May 2002, of the number of successful candidates of the selection for salary scale 20E. If the result of this calculation were less favourable than the application of the old social programming standard (8% of the total number of posts in the career), the old programming standard remains valid. A recalculation of the number of posts for access to salary scale 22B is no longer possible, given the extinction character of this measure. C. Communication of the definitively fixed number of posts
Soon you will be informed by letter of the definitive number of posts for the salary scales at level D and for salary scale 22B, so that you can take the necessary actions to make the promotions in the new salary scales up to this number and according to the exact criteria (notably following the language legislation). These promotions cannot be granted before 1 October 2002 given that the royal decree of 5 September 2002 reforming the career of certain officials of the State administrations was published at the end of September 2002. The Minister of the Public Service
and the Modernisation of the administration,
L. VAN DEN BOSSCHE

-	the number of posts determined at that time in the ministerial order implementing the staff establishment in the salary scales subject to standards, except in case of surplus. In that case, the number of statutory staff on 31 December 2001 is taken into consideration. In order to know the definitive number of posts per salary scale in level D, the following social programming standards are applied to this total:
1. for the grade of administrative collaborator:
-	27% in salary scale DA2; -	31% in salary scale DA3; -	12% in salary scale DA4; 2. for the grade of technical collaborator:
-	25% in salary scale DT3; -	35% in salary scale DT4; -	10% in salary scale DT5. If the number of persons as a result of integration into the new career were to exceed the number of posts per salary scale, it remains a case of surplus. The surplus therefore does not become the new standard. A recalculation of these numbers is only possible following significant staff transfers in the framework of a restructuring or transfer of competences or following negotiations on particular grades insofar as they are integrated into the common grades. B. Social programming standard for access to salary scale 22B
The officials who are integrated on 1 June 2002 into the new salary scales and who at the time of integration are remunerated in salary scales 20E and 22A, may, as a transitional measure, be promoted to salary scale 22B in extinction. This promotion to 22B in extinction depends on the number of vacant posts provided for in this salary scale. The total number of posts linked to this salary scale 22B is fixed at 25% of the number of permanently appointed officials on 31 May 2002, remunerated in salary scales 20E, 22A and 22B, and, after 31 May 2002, of the number of successful candidates for the selection for salary scale 20E. If the result of this calculation were to be less favourable than the application of the former social programming standard (8% of the total number of posts in the career), the former social programming standard remains valid. A recalculation of the number of posts for access to salary scale 22B is no longer possible, given the extinction character of this measure. C. Communication of the definitively fixed number of posts
Soon you will be informed, by letter, of the definitive number of posts for the salary scales of level D and for salary scale 22B, so that you can undertake the necessary actions in order to promote the staff up to these numbers and according to the correct criteria (with inter alia compliance with the language legislation). These promotions cannot be granted before 1 October 2002 since the royal decree of 5 September 2002 reforming the career of certain officials of the State Administrations was published at the end of September 2002. The Minister of Public Service Affairs
and Modernisation of the public administrations,
L. VAN DEN BOSSCHE

GHENT UNIVERSITY

Independent academic staff

In the Faculty of Sciences, from 1 October 2003, a full-time position of lecturer or senior lecturer is to be filled in the department of Geology and soil science for a task comprising academic teaching, scientific research and scientific services in the field of palaeontology. Profile:
holder of the diploma of doctor in sciences: earth and mineral resources group or geology; provide proof of research activity in the field of micro-palaeontology of the Meso- and Cenozoic. This must be substantiated by recent publications in scientific journals that have wide distribution and that call upon experts for the assessment of submitted manuscripts; possess the necessary didactic and communicative skills and experience to provide academic teaching in palaeontology and biosphere evolution. This full-time position gives in principle rise to a permanent appointment, without prejudice to the possibility that the university board in the case of first appointment as member of the independent academic staff may proceed to an appointment in a temporary employment relationship for a duration of at most three years with a view to a permanent appointment after that period, if the university board assesses the performance of the person concerned favourably. Depending on the specific profile of the selected candidate, the grade of senior lecturer or lecturer is granted. Applications must be submitted in duplicate, by registered letter, addressed to the rector of Ghent University, Sint-Pietersnieuwstraat 25, 9000 Ghent, on the forms provided for that purpose for ZAP accompanied by the certificates of competence (copies of diplomas), within a period of thirty days to be counted from the publication of this notice in the Belgian Official Gazette. The application forms for ZAP can be obtained at the address of Ghent University, attn. the directorate of personnel and organisation, section administration of statutory staff, Sint-Pietersnieuwstraat 25, 9000 Ghent, or requested by telephone on no. 09-264 31 29 or
09-264 31 30. They are also made available on the internet, URL: http://www.rug.ac.be/RUGportal/nl/personeel/personeelsaangel/   formulieren/ZAP	(19082)

Municipality of Beernem

Special development plans — Public inquiry

The college of mayor and aldermen of the municipality of Beernem brings to the general knowledge of interested parties, in accordance with the provisions of the decree concerning spatial planning coordinated on 22 October 1996, that the draft S.D.P. "Oudevaartstraat" was provisionally accepted in the session of 16 December 2002 of the municipal council. This S.D.P. is available for inspection by everyone for thirty days, at the municipal hall of Beernem, Bloemendalestraat 112, technical service office, all working days from 9 to 12 o'clock, from Monday 17 February 2003. Any objections or comments must be submitted in writing to the college of mayor and aldermen and received before the closing date of the public inquiry, Wednesday 19 March 2003. Beernem, 4 February 2003. (3164)

Municipality of Heusden-Zolder

The municipal authority of Heusden-Zolder is a dynamic and colourful public authority. We strive to provide services to our citizens in a contemporary, simple and customer-friendly manner. To help us achieve our objectives, with a view to establishing a recruitment reserve, we are looking for a m/f for our technical department of public works:
Mechanic. As a mechanic you will be responsible for the maintenance of the municipal vehicle fleet. You therefore have a good understanding of car and truck mechanics. You can also work independently and have a sense of initiative. Interested? Send your application letter with curriculum vitae by registered mail no later than 28 February (the postmark serves as proof) to: Municipal Authority Heusden-Zolder, Heldenplein 1, 3550 Heusden-Zolder. Municipality of Duffel

Is recruiting:
A second lieutenant in the professional staff of the fire service (PBA 1). Job description:
Under the authority of the head of service, he/she shares responsibility for the management of the fire brigade. Under the direction of the head of service, he/she is jointly responsible for the organisation, coordination, planning and proper functioning of the service in accordance with the legal provisions in this regard. The second lieutenant may be promoted to the rank of lieutenant if he/she meets all the conditions set out in the royal decree of 19 April 1999 and following. Admission and recruitment conditions:
1. Have conduct in accordance with the requirements of the position sought. 2. Enjoy civil and political rights. 3. Comply with the military service laws (for male candidates). 4. Be physically fit. 5. Possess Belgian nationality. 6. Be at least 1.60 m tall for male candidates and 1.55 m for female candidates. 7. Diploma:
Required diploma as mentioned in the annex to the royal decree of 19 April 1999 and following establishing the aptitude and competence criteria as well as the appointment and promotion conditions for officers of public fire services. Be in possession of the certificates as determined in the royal decree of 19 April 1999 and following, or obtain these diplomas/certificates during the probationary period. 8. Hold a CE driving licence or obtain it during the probationary period. 9. Age: minimum 21 years on the date of 15 March 2003. 10. Resident in the municipality of Duffel, no later than 6 months after the date of permanent appointment. 11. Pass a recruitment examination. Your applications with the addition of a detailed curriculum vitae, as well as copies of the required diplomas and certificates, must be addressed by registered letter or against receipt, to the College of Mayor and Aldermen, Gemeentestraat 21, 2570 Duffel, no later than 15 March 2003. The complete job description and profile and the examination programme can be obtained at the town hall, personnel department (tel. 015-30 72 43/47/49) or e-mail: rudi.gillis@publilink.be)
Further information regarding the content of the task can be obtained from Lt. Paul De Clerck (tel. 015-31 36 66 or e-mail: brandweer.duffel@kbbf.be) (3176)

Public Centre for Social Welfare of Machelen

The P.C.S.W. Machelen is recruiting for its rest home (82 beds): in statutory capacity:
1 FT nurse C3 (certified) 1 FT nurse BV1 (graduate) 1 FT assistant cook D1
in contractual capacity (for indefinite duration): 1 FT elderly care assistant C1
Job description nurses:
care and nursing of the elderly; monitoring the general nutritional status of residents; social support of the elderly; guiding and supporting elderly care assistants in their tasks; administrative and logistical tasks. Job description elderly care assistant:
care of the elderly; support in nursing the elderly; assistance with meals for the elderly; social support of the elderly; administrative and logistical tasks. Job description assistant cook:
preparing and serving meals; storing deliveries, foodstuffs and leftovers; maintaining the kitchen; coordinating the organisation of the kitchen in the absence of the head cook; supporting tasks. Special recruitment conditions:
nurse C3: hold a diploma of certified nurse; nurse BV1: hold a diploma of higher education of the short type, graduate in nursing; elderly care assistant C1: hold a diploma, certificate or attestation of HSBO family or elderly care, or equivalent; assistant cook D1: hold a diploma of lower secondary education or equivalent. Recruitment examinations will be organised and recruitment reserves of 3 years established. Main general admission and recruitment conditions: Be Belgian or a national of an EU member state. Have good moral character and be physically fit. Comply with the military service laws (for male candidates). Be 18 years old on 7 March 2003. We offer:
Good salary. Financial valorisation of private seniority for nurses and elderly care assistant. Meal vouchers. Free hospitalisation insurance. Contribution to transport costs when using public transport for home-work travel. Bicycle allowance. Extensive training opportunities. How to apply:
Send your application no later than 7 March 2003 (postmark date) to the P.C.S.W. Machelen, C. Peetersstraat 45, 1830 Machelen, with the accompanying supporting documents:
a curriculum vitae; proof of good moral character (max. 3 months old); an extract from the birth certificate; a copy of the required educational certificate; a family composition (to be obtained from the population service of the municipality); a military certificate (for male candidates). For further information you can contact, during office hours, Mrs Veerle Van Vaerenbergh (acting secretary): 02-756 55 23. (3177)

Municipality of Ranst

The municipal authority of Ranst is proceeding with the recruitment of (m/f):
1 sports promoter
statutory, half-time with recruitment reserve of 3 years (m/f) Function:
initiating, promoting and supporting sports activities in the municipality, continuing sports activities on Wednesday afternoons, providing leadership to teachers and in the organisation of events, together with the sports officer and from their own perspective preparing sports policy and advising the sports council and the College of Mayor and Aldermen, monitoring the quality and quantity of services provided. Requirements:
hold the diploma of lower secondary education teacher in physical education or teacher in physical education; candidates must pass a recruitment examination organised by the municipality, basic knowledge of the municipal law and specific basic legislation specific to the field, have insight into the operation and organisation of the municipality, good knowledge of the use of information technology, customer-friendly attitude, team spirit and collegiality, ability to take initiative, flexibility, variable working hours (sometimes evening or weekend work). Level: level B1 - B3 (annual salary of € 11,189.64). 1 urban planning officer
statutory, full-time with recruitment reserve of 3 years (m/f) Function:
providing support to the head of department of spatial planning and environment regarding work organisation, being responsible for service provision in the field of urban planning and spatial planning, helping to develop a coherent vision and translating it into advice and policy-preparatory reports to the policy bodies, ensuring correct implementation of the tasks assigned by the legislation on spatial planning, including the applicable implementing decrees, correct monitoring of legislation on urban planning and spatial planning. Requirements:
hold a diploma giving access to level B and a diploma from a spatial planning course as determined in the ministerial decree of 13 July 2000; candidates must pass a recruitment examination organised by the municipality, receive a favourable assessment after an assessment on leadership, thorough knowledge of the decree of 18 May 1999 on the organisation of spatial planning and subsequent amendments and the implementing decrees, basic knowledge of municipal legislation, independence from external influence, ability to work under time pressure, flexibility, customer-friendliness. Level: level B1 - B3 (indexed annual salary of € 22,379.28). Benefits: meal vouchers, hospitalisation insurance, hearth or residence allowance. Interested? Applications must be addressed to the mayor of Ranst, Gustaaf Peetersstraat 7, 2520 Ranst, and must be received no later than 10 March 2003. The following documents must be attached: legalised copy of the required diploma, proof of good conduct and morals and military certificate for male candidates (more information can be obtained from the municipality's personnel department). (3289)

Publication prescribed by article 793 of the Civil Code
Bekendmaking voorgeschreven bij artikel 793 van het Burgerlijk Wetboek

Acceptance subject to inventory Aanvaarding onder voorrecht van boedelbeschrijving

By deed drawn up at the registry of the court of first instance in Bruges, on 31 January 2003, declared, Mr Dirk Delbaere, notarial assistant, residing at Melle, Kloosterstraat 41, acting as special attorney pursuant to a private power of attorney of 14 January 2003, on behalf of Mrs Warmoes, Els Paula Maria, born in Bruges on 22 July 1971, residing at Bruges (Assebroek), Van Steenestraat 68, acting as surviving parent over Verhamme, Sarah Julia Leonie, born in Ostend on 10 April 1994, residing at Bruges (Assebroek), Van Steenestraat 68, to accept in the name and on behalf of the minor child, the estate, subject to inventory, of the late Mr Pierre Francesco Verhamme, born in Ostend on 12 August 1966, last residing at De Haan (Vlissegem), Warvinge 40, and deceased in De Haan, on 2 July 2002. Mrs Els Warmoes is authorized to accept this estate on behalf of the minor child, Sarah Verhamme, subject to inventory, by order of the justice of the peace of the first canton in Bruges, on 2 January 2003. The creditors and legatees are requested, within three months from the publication in the Belgian Official Gazette, to make their claims and rights known by registered letter to notary Patrick Van Hoestenberghe, at 8490 Jabbeke, Gistelsteenweg 295. Drawn up in Jabbeke on 5 February 2003. P. Van Hoestenberghe, notary. (3180)

Court of First Instance in Leuven

On 27 January 2003, at the registry of the court of first instance in Leuven; Before us, D. De Coster, deputy clerk of the same court, appeared Broos, Sofie Maria Georges, born in Diest on 8 February 1976, residing at 3290 Diest, Bevrijdingsstraat 31/2, acting in her capacity as mother, holder of parental authority over her minor child: De Waele, Sander Blanche, born in Diest on 30 March 1999, residing with the mother. The appearing party declares to us, acting in her said capacity, to accept subject to inventory the estate of the late De Waele, Koenraad Renaat Maria Juliaan, born in Diest on 2 May 1971, in life last residing at 3290 Diest, Drieharingenstraat 2/B002, and deceased in Diest on 9 September 2002. In support of her declaration, the appearing party showed us a copy of the order of the justice of the peace of the canton of Diest, dated 20 January 2003 whereby she was authorized to accept in the name of the aforementioned minor De Waele, Sander, the estate of the late De Waele, Koenraad Renaat Maria Juliaan, subject to inventory. She also declared to us to elect, in this procedure, and by application of article 793 of the Civil Code, domicile at the office of Mr Luc Bogaerts, notary at 3290 Diest, Graanmarkt 1, where the creditors and legatees must make themselves known. Whereof deed, which the appearing party, after reading, signed with us, deputy clerk. (Illegible signatures). (3181)

Commercial Court of Brussels

Mr Breckpot, Pierre, born in Aalst on 13 June 1925, residing at 9300 Aalst, Graanmarkt 11, acting as attorney of Mrs Aelbrecht, Martine Anitta, born in Meldert on 21 April 1957, acting in the capacity of mother over the minor still residing with her being:

Commercial Court in Mechelen

By judgment dated 5 February 2003 of the commercial court in Mechelen, on confession, Mr. De Wachter, Jan Bertha, operator of horticultural business-tomato nursery, born in Duffel on 29 June 1969, residing at 2570 Duffel, Euster 88, not registered in H.R. Mechelen, VAT 744.204.388, was declared bankrupt. The date of cessation of payments was fixed at 5 February 2003. As judge-commissioner was appointed: G. Volckaerts, judge in commercial matters. As curator was appointed: Mr. Verdoodt, Robert, lawyer, with office at 2800 Mechelen, 't Vlietje 16. Declaration of claims must be made within thirty days from 5 February 2003, at the registry of the commercial court at 2800 Mechelen, Voochtstraat 7. For certified true copy: the clerk, (signed) R. Cabuy. (3280)

Matrimonial Regime − Huwelijksvermogensstelsel

By judgment of the seventh chamber of the court of first instance in Brussels dated 17 December 2002, the deed of modification of matrimonial regime executed before notary Paul Van Holen, in Galmaarden, on 28 September 2002, between Mr. Coppens, René Frans Rosa, with national number 600814-331-80, born in Ninove on 14 August 1960, and his spouse, Mrs. Wellekens, Martine Micheline, with national number 600702-250-29, born in Aalst on 2 July 1960, residing together at 1750 Galmaarden, Bruinhoekstraat 27, was approved. This deed contains the modification of their statutory regime to the regime of separation of property in accordance with article 1394 et seq. of the Civil Code. On behalf of the applicants, (signed) Paul Van Holen, notary. (3203)

By judgment of the seventh chamber of the court of first instance in Brussels dated 17 December 2002, the deed of modification of matrimonial regime executed before notary Paul Van Holen, in Galmaarden on 24 October 2002, between Mr. Krikilion, Werner Arthur Ludo, born in Ghent on 27 July 1961, and his spouse, Mrs. Becqué, Betty, born in Ninove on 7 September 1966, residing together at 1570 Galmaarden (Tollembeek), Hernestraat 27, was approved. This deed contains the maintenance of the statutory regime but with contribution of immovable property of Mr. Werner Krikilion to the common property Krikilion-Becqué. On behalf of the applicants, (signed) Paul Van Holen, notary. (3204)

By judgment of the court of first instance in Ieper on 20 December 2002, the deed executed before notary Jo Debyser, on 9 October 2002, containing modification of the matrimonial regime between Mr. Crombez, Patrick Marcel, farmer and horticulturist, and his spouse, Mrs. Vanlerberghe, Ann, agricultural assistant, residing together at 8840 Staden, Klerkenstraat 18, was approved. The approval requested of the notarial deed executed before notary Michel Nolens, in Lille, on 16 December 2002, in which it is stipulated that the clause included in the deed of modification of matrimonial regime executed before notary Michel Nolens, in Lille, on 7 June 1979, is to lapse. By petition of 12 December 2002 Mr. Couckuyt, Ronny Michel Georges, self-employed, and his spouse, Mrs. Elyn, Annick Cecile Joseph, employee, residing together at 9070 Destelbergen, Admiraalhof 68, married under the statutory regime for lack of prior marriage contract, requested from the court of first instance in Ghent approval of the deed executed on 12 December 2002 before notary Philippe Kluyskens, in Sint-Amandsberg, now Ghent, containing modification of their matrimonial regime to pure separation of property. Ghent (Sint-Amandsberg), 6 February 2003. For the applicants, (signed) Philippe Kluyskens, notary. (3206)

By petition of 27 January 2003, the spouses, Mr. Van Dongen, Gerard Johanna Dorothea, born in Nijmegen (Netherlands), on 26 November 1930, of Dutch nationality, and his spouse, Mrs. Yen, Jeannine Raymonde, born in Paris (France), on 19 December 1930, of French nationality, both residing at 3090 Overijse, Spitherlaan 2, filed before the civil court of first instance in Brussels a request for approval of the deed of modification of their matrimonial regime, executed before notary Maryelle Van den Moortel, in Overijse, on 27 January 2003. The spouses are currently married under the regime of separation of property pursuant to marriage contract executed before notary André Nerincx, in Brussels on 23 December 1971. Through the deed of modification of matrimonial regime Mr. Van Dongen, Gerard, contributed immovable property, which belongs to him personally, to an added internal common property. Overijse, 5 February 2003. (Signed) M. Van den Moortel, notary. (3207)

By virtue of a judgment pronounced by the first civil chamber of the court of first instance of Dinant, on 19 December 2002, the deed containing modification of the matrimonial regime between the spouses Pruys Alain/Neumann Rolande, domiciled at 5600 Philippeville (Franchimont), rue Mont Coliat 26, being the deed drawn up by notary Jacques Dubuc of Philippeville dated 25 October 2002, was approved in the following terms:

For Mr and Mrs Santamaria Duran-Croes, (signed) Erik Struyf, notary. (3215)
